## Ch. 8 resolved design (log of decisions, session 7)
- **Decisions:** One first day, continuous from Ch. 7's gate, routine compressed in coda (implied-time principle; deliberate structural rhyme with Ch. 3 — the mountain's curriculum in one day / the Temple's world in one day). The cover performance = the roll (registry book, the chains vs. the blank column; "Empty is true" — Severin's mouth, valve register; no respend of "Kael of what?"). The near-slip runs on the SWALLOWED WORD (steps around "lazy" like the third board — register hygiene predating the hiding arc). Priscian's "Everyone has a fire" carries the complication. Aurelian's cruelty fails to register via the body's arithmetic ("nothing here is heavy"); his read left as an unnamed drawer. Elarine spent as exactly two silent crumbs. Oss hands out the diminutive (the intimacy-system inverse — sealed in 02/03). The streaked master = one sentence, image only. Settlement roofs = one clause. Group laughter fired twice, tells only. THE HOMECOMING (added on author direction): love visible, never stated — the seed-bread for the boy, the lower-pens wait, the inversion supper, the escaped sound, the carried-across-ice hand; counterweight intact (nothing softens; the third school lands on the child's evening like one more yoke even while rendered as warmth; the worry runs under the pride in the loom-glance and the stopped question). THE ROUTINE canonized (author direction): dawn = Vask (weapons + bow; combat-magic braid reserved post-declaration — Vask's lightning sealed in 00 with the camouflage function and the coordinated leash); daylight = Temple; lamplight = Neris's evening school. The brutal day = deliberate, rendered once without comment.

## SESSION 18 ADDENDUM 6 — 2026-08-21 (THE 07 FOLDER SPLIT + THE PROTOCOL FOLD + THE 05 CLEANUP, author-directed: "how do we handle 04, 05, 07")
- **Framing that drove all three:** every file had an intake rule and no EVICTION rule — content arrived when relevant and never left when it stopped being. Each of 04/05/07 was given a one-line contract, an admission test, and a named exit.
- **07 → `07-Story-Ledger/` (folder), split on GROWTH BEHAVIOUR rather than topic.** Measured before deciding: chapter records accumulate ~728w per chapter, protected phrasings ~157w per chapter (two unbounded accumulators, ~885w/chapter combined); timeline + standing rules + magic + open threads + established facts total 2,385w and are asymptotic. At Book One's ~20 chapters 07 would have been ~25k words, ~70% accumulator. A single split would have isolated one accumulator and needed redoing within a book. Structure: LAW CORE (timeline, standing-rules, planted-clues, magic-snapshot, open-threads, established-facts) + ACCUMULATORS (chapter-records, protected-phrasings) + index.md (pure TOC) + changelog.md.
- **07's first eviction rule (new standing rules, logged in the 07 index):** (1) PER-BOOK ROLL — at each book boundary the accumulators roll to `chapter-records-book-N.md` / `protected-phrasings-book-N.md` and leave the every-session read; the law core never moves. (2) TIERING — skip a chapter's entry in `chapter-records.md` when that chapter is being read in full from `manuscript/` (~2,000w/session of duplication under the tiered cold read, growing every chapter).
- **`scripts/check-echoes.sh` repointed** to `07-Story-Ledger/protected-phrasings.md`. PARITY VERIFIED EXACT: 213 quoted spans parsed before and after; full-manuscript run and all 14 per-chapter runs byte-identical to the pre-split baseline.
- **Verification:** all 400 non-empty lines of the pre-split 07 found verbatim in the folder; the single exception is the old status line, rolled to this log (§07 STATUS LINE) and superseded by index.md. Pre-split file at git 40bca1c.
- **The working protocol FOLDED INTO CLAUDE.md** and removed from 05. Rationale: 548w of rules that change almost never sat in the file that churns hardest — that adjacency is why the status chain kept swelling. CLAUDE.md auto-loads every session, so the rules now cost zero cold-read budget, and CLAUDE.md's §Session protocol no longer defers to a cold-read file ("full rules live in 05 — they win" was a pointer running backwards). Process homes: 2 → 1. Verbatim pre-fold text below.
- **05 reduced to LIVE STATE.** Status chain rewritten pointer-style per the session-18 rule (which had been honoured in the letter, violated in the spirit — the s18 entry alone was ~600w of chapter retelling); tic reports rolled here; baselines kept. 2,728 → ~700 words. Contract written into the file: "if it will still be true in ten sessions, it does not belong here."
- **Duplication resolved:** the standing queued-threads list now lives ONLY in 04 §Queued threads; 05 §NEXT TARGET points at it instead of restating it.

### 05 §Working Protocol — VERBATIM pre-fold text (rolled at the protocol fold; distilled into CLAUDE.md)
## Working Protocol (sessions)
- **STATUS-CHAIN HYGIENE (standing rules, session-18 compaction, author-ratified scope):** (1) the §Status chain keeps only the last 2–3 sessions inline; at each session close the oldest inline "Prior:" block rolls VERBATIM to archive/session-log.md §"05 STATUS-CHAIN ROLL-OFF". (2) Status entries are POINTER-STYLE henceforth: "Ch. N committed — full record in 07" + session-specific state only (rulings, hygiene, next target); no fourth retelling of chapter contents — 07 owns the one canonical per-chapter record.
- **Two chapters of new prose per session; three is the hard ceiling** (ceiling only for structurally simple chapters). Heavy-canon chapters (the persuasion, the tournament, the fire) get a session to themselves — with a planning session beforehand if needed.
- **Rhythm (amended at migration, 2026-08-20):** open on `git status` clean → draft → react/revise while hot → approve → commit decisions to 05 → `git commit` → close. Working files ARE the canon; there is no reupload step.
- **Revision passes are cheap:** the ceiling applies to NEW prose, not line-edits or polish of existing chapters.
- **Nothing lives in chat history:** every decision, rule, or promising unresolved idea gets written into a file before session end. Chat transcripts are disposable scaffolding.
- **Each session opens with a cold read of full canon** — treat it as a free continuity audit; flag anything that itches.
- **VERSION CONTROL IS THE SAFETY NET (replaces FINAL HANDOFF ONCE at migration, 2026-08-20 — that rule and its stale-snapshot hygiene existed because Claude Chat had no versioning):** every session closes with a `git commit` (short imperative subject). Session-open verification = `git status` must be clean; anything modified or untracked that no log entry explains → STOP and report. The wc-w baselines in this file remain as a cheap cross-check and as the flat-or-leaner budget, but git history is the authority on what changed. Cut material (e.g. the Ch. 4 wind exchange) is recoverable from history — restoration really is one edit now.
- **COLD-READ SCALING (amendment, session 7):** the full cold read (all bibles + all chapters) holds as written through ~Ch. 12. Claude is responsible for flagging the session it starts to strain; either party may call it. When called, switch to the TIERED COLD READ: (1) all bibles in full, every session, forever — they are the compressed truth and must stay fully loaded; (2) the most recent 2–3 chapters in full, non-negotiable — the maturing-voice gradient is calibrated locally, chapter against neighbors; no summary carries cadence; (3) older chapters via their 05 summaries, with targeted spot-reads of any chapter the session will touch (an echo, a protected phrasing, a scene being rhymed against). The bible system is the insurance that makes this safe: continuity lives in the established facts, protected phrasings, and seed tables (in 07-Story-Ledger.md since the split) — never in rereading the manuscript. HYGIENE AT THE BOOK ONE BOUNDARY: freeze the Part I chapter summaries into a separate archive file so 05 stays a workhorse, not a haybarn. (EXECUTED EARLY at the 2026-08-20 split — and generalized: law lives in 07, history in archive/session-log.md.) **TIERED COLD READ CALLED (session 15, author-ratified): in effect from session 16 on** — all bibles in full every session; the most recent 2–3 chapters in full; older chapters via 07 summaries + targeted spot-reads of anything the session touches.

## SESSION 18 ADDENDUM 7 — 2026-08-21 (07 LAW-CORE TERSE REWRITE, author-flagged: "reads as not to the point")
- **Trigger:** the author flagged the law core for carrying discarded/brainstormed material as if it were canon — the specimen being established-facts' `Released:` bullet. Measured session/date-stamp density before deciding: established-facts 9 stamps in 532w (1 per 59w), standing-rules 8 in 1,147w, timeline 2 in 458w, magic-snapshot 1 in 257w, planted-clues 5 in 5,128w, open-threads 0. Scope set to the top four; planted-clues (a proper table with a Status column) and open-threads (48w of pure fact) were already right and were NOT touched.
- **Register faults fixed:** (1) session/date stamps on facts — provenance belongs to the changelog and this log; (2) spend/discard narrative where only the current state is operative (the `SPENT session 7: Aurelian (→ Corvane)` chains restate outcomes already stated as fact); (3) cross-file duplication — released names carried in BOTH established-facts and magic-snapshot; the resonance five-term register restated from 02's lexicon; the Temple cast roster restated from 03's index.
- **Unlike the day's earlier passes this was a REWRITE of do-not-contradict canon, not a move** — words genuinely disappear. Discipline applied: verbatim originals rolled here first (below); an operative checklist (every name, blocked term, age pin, and rule) enumerated before and compared after; every dropped span verified present in 03/02/00 or confirmed archaeology.

### 07-Story-Ledger/established-facts.md — VERBATIM pre-rewrite text
## Established Facts (do not contradict)
**LAW CORE.** Do-not-contradict facts that no single chapter owns.

(Per-chapter established-facts blocks merged into §Chapter Records at the session-18 compaction — nothing dropped: unique content moved verbatim into each chapter's FACTS tail, duplicates of the summary/clue table deduplicated. General facts below.)
- Names: Kael (no known surname); Valeria Vel (Line of Vel, 11 generations of royal protectors); Aeliana Cassivara Vessantine (the princess; House Vessantine; the empire is the Vessantine Empire); Vask (father); Neris (mother); Wren (brother); Severin (the master; publicly THE FIRST MASTER); "the Gardener" (Kael's pattern-name for the leader — true name reserved, see 00). **Temple cast (sealed sessions 7–8):** Ostrian "Oss" Vessaline (almost-friend; merchant house, bought ornament); Aurelian Corvane (ringleader; FIRE proficiency sealed session 8); Elarine Sedrane (the watcher; collegial register sealed); Priscian (master of natural philosophy); RUTHEN (the yard-master — named session 8; the streaked master); LUCAN DRUSANE (the worker of Aurelian's four; house Drusane).
- Bloodline descends through NERIS (see 00): she was the program's harvested ASSET; Vask was a forged INSTRUMENT. Kael and Wren carry it through her.
- Parents' names are CHOSEN, not given — instruments/assets had designations (see 00). No "real" names exist beneath Vask and Neris.
- "Neris" belongs to no naming register; its true nature is sealed (see 00). Never explain it on the page.
- Severin is mostly "the master" on-page; the name appears rarely, by design.
- Naming device: Aeliana's name shortens as her empire recedes (full chain → "Aeliana" → "Liana"/"Ana"); first shortening is a reserved beat.
- Released: "Rhaenya" (Rhaenyra collision); "Aelin" (Throne of Glass); **"magicule" (Slime collision — mechanic kept, term replaced by "the minima")**; **"Dain" (Tolkien collision — released from the guard-register pool, session 7)**. SPENT session 7: Aurelian (→ Corvane), Elarine (→ Sedrane), Vessaline (→ merchant house). SPENT session 8: Ruthen (→ the yard-master); Lucan Drusane (→ house Drusane; the -ANE ending embraced as the imperial-house convention, see 02). RELEASED session 8: Helvane (-vane collision with Corvane), Ostade (Os- collision with Oss/Ostrian). Unspent imperial candidates: Cassivara-as-standalone. Banked: "vessence" (propaganda register only). SEALED bible-only term (session 8): the Sanctum (00 — never on the page). SESSION 14 — THE RESONANCE REFORM: adopted five-term magic register (02 lexicon): Resonant (person) · resonance (the person↔minima CONNECTION) · resonate (establishing it) · resonant thread (a controlled connection — the "spend a thread" idiom's formal term) · Resonance Theory (the scholarly field). RELEASED: "wright" (superseded), "channeling" (Wheel of Time collision); "mage" retired from page vocabulary; "working" returned to ordinary labor. Retro pass run on Ch. 8–10 + all bibles same session.
- Kael: social blindness flaw; technician talent (low raw output, exceptional control/tactics).
- Father teaches body; mother teaches mind/magic; parents never seen fighting until the fire; father dies in the fire.
- Mother gives birth (brother) at end of Act One; extracted with master; both unconfirmed thereafter.
- The trio escapes: Kael + Valeria + the princess. Book Two opens with them.
- The raiders are COALITION forces; Kael's initial truth: they are the enemy.
- The master placed the parents on the island; brought Kael to the Temple as "the master's charity case."
- Kael doesn't learn surnames exist until the Temple.

### 07-Story-Ledger/timeline.md — VERBATIM pre-rewrite text
## Timeline (planned)
**LAW CORE.** Planned part/age structure and every age pinned on the page.

- Part I: Kael ~4–10 (ends with permission + enrollment) · Part II: ~10–12 (Temple years, peers, BEFORE Valeria) · Part III: ~12–13 (Valeria) · Part IV: ~13–14 (tournament, birth of brother, the fire).
- **Ages pinned on the page:** Ch. 1 = 4 ("four years old, rich with certainty"). Ch. 2 = 5 ("last summer" = the age-4 season). Ch. 3 = 8 ("He was eight, and he had theories") — deliberate 3-year jump; the routine implies the elapsed years. Ch. 4 = 9 ("He was nine, and the rules lived in his body now"). Ch. 5 = 9 through the year; crossing at midwinter, "nine years and a winter old". Ch. 6 = same night as the Ch. 5 crossing ("a nine-year-old boy in a yard of grown men") — continuous action, no time jump. Ch. 7 = midwinter healing → spring conversation → autumn departure; "ten years old" pinned twice at the Temple gate (birthday passes unmarked inside the year — deliberate; never pin the date). Ch. 8 = 10, same autumn morning, continuous from the gate ("He was ten" pinned in the coda); coda compresses into routine reaching "the turn of winter." Ch. 9 = 10 (age not re-pinned on page — continuous first Temple year); spans winter term → spring (movements in thematic, not strict chronological, order — deliberate; session 13: the close no longer carries a season stamp — the chapter now ends in the loft after the candle audit, on the wool crackle; Ch. 10 still opens at winter's turn and now supplies the trial's construction in its own opening). Ch. 10 = "The Asking" (redrafted + committed session 14): Kael 10 throughout (no age pin; the birthday passes unmarked between chapters), turn toward spring → the summer's turn of the first Temple year. Ch. 11 = "The Dish": "He was eleven" pinned at the ceremony; second autumn → the cold term. Ch. 12 = "The Telling": Kael 11 (not re-pinned; "He would be thirteen" at the two-springs horizon carries the arithmetic); storm-weeks of the second cold term → the turn toward spring; the pan-away nine days after the announcement. Ch. 13 = "The Coat": Kael 11→12 (birthday unmarked, per rule); the full year from the announcement's spring → the following cold term ("at the spring, one year would remain"); Ch. 14 = "The Measure": Kael 12 (never pinned on-page — carried by "a girl his own age" + the Ch. 13 arithmetic); opens at the spring one year out (the advance's three ships, "the first fair week of the turn"); arrival, exhibition, and duel run continuous within that spring; the year-before-tournament opens here (Part III).

### 07-Story-Ledger/standing-rules.md — VERBATIM pre-rewrite text
## Standing Rules (from author)
**LAW CORE.** Author-ratified rules that govern how the prose is written.

- **Love is embedded in the teaching, never stated.** Every stern lesson must carry the signature of protection in its stitching (the hand before the ground; the lie that makes a horizon). If a teaching scene reads stern without detectable love, revision flag.
- **FATHER-GLIMPSE LEDGER (ration hard).** Sanctioned glimpses of Vask's true capacity spent: (1) the slate, Ch. 1; (2) rising out of the nothing, Ch. 2; (3) the wrist catch, Ch. 3. Ch. 4 and Ch. 5: zero spent. EXCEPTION EXECUTED (Ch. 6): the rescue rendered ONLY as fragments through half-conscious POV (sound stopped mid-note; absence; hands; "a quiet the size of several men") — Kael could never testify to what happened; no comparison frame built (deliberately: the reader never sees Vask do what Kael couldn't). The account holds at three-plus-fragments and is now VERY expensive. No new glimpses until the "never seen them try" beat (mid-book). The fire remains the only full answer.
- **THE KYNEMIR ABSORBED-STATE RULE (sealed in 00, session 5 — page-facing consequence):** the blood surfaces from the absorbed/flow state, NEVER from panic or fear. Ch. 6's beating therefore correctly shows no surfacing. Any future scene of Kael in mortal terror must NOT flicker; any future absorbed-state scene is live territory. The fire candidate depends on him being past panic, into something else.
- **GROUP LAUGHTER IS NOW A CHARGED REGISTER FOR KAEL (Ch. 6 onward, unmarked).** Every future scene where people laugh around him — the Temple yards, feasts, the golden children — has the settlement under it. Never labeled; render as his small physical tells only. Its term of art on the page: **"a crop of laughter"** (ratified session 13 as deliberate coinage — logged in 02's lexicon; four instances Ch. 8–10; further uses deliberate-only). Ch. 6 revision (session 13) extended the register's origin: the men's laughter now runs the full night in three strikes (the spotting, their own man down, "someone was laughing still").
- **SCIENCE REGISTER — AMENDED TO A GRADIENT (session 6; full rule in 02).** The permanent ban is on OUT-OF-WORLD vocabulary only (no modern terms, no formulas as notation). Knowledge, method, and precision deepen WITHOUT CEILING across the series. Part I = craft-lore (holds as written). Part II+ = the HONESTY PIVOT (canonized Ch. 7): the parents teach with precision where they can afford it; Kael's language matures into genuine period natural philosophy; he coins his own terms where the world has none (far-counting precedent — the lexicon habit is a canonized trait, see 03); the EXPERIMENTAL TURN begins (his own trials — "asking twice with one thing changed"). Sealed irony (00): honesty in five schools, rationing in three (their past, history, magic) — the pivot draws the map of their secrets in negative space; Kael will notice the asymmetry (filed unknowing Ch. 7 — Part II beat). If a lesson reads MODERN, revision flag; if it reads merely precise, that is now correct.
- **THREE STACKED SYSTEMS (Part II engine, session 6, canon — promoted from 04 at the session-18 addendum 5 (the outline compaction)).** Kael arrives at the Temple carrying three systems at once: ignorance + settlement fear + armor; the golden children read the composite as strangeness. His threat-calibration (set in the yard) makes their worst fail to register as danger; his calm under mockery enrages; escalation spiral. Runs unlabeled under every Temple-yard scene.
- **CRACKED WASTER — INERT FOREVER (ruled session 12; promoted from 04's superseded brief at the session-18 addendum 5 (the outline compaction)).** The waster with the seated crack (Ch. 9, Lucan's rig) is spent: the crack-up return WAS the resolution. Do not bring it back as a device.
- **THE WEAPONS SCHOOL (opened Ch. 7).** Sword, spear, bow — on WASTERS (Vask-carved ash; steel is banked — the Temple's, or the fire's). Runs at home in parallel with the Temple years (mornings stay Vask's), powering the concealment thread from both ends: the Temple never sees what the mountain teaches; the mountain never asks what he shows the Temple. Vask's Ch. 2 register holds (beside him, longer, better; corrections one stroke). At-capacity rule untouched.
- **THE MENTOR THREAD (opened Ch. 7 — develop gradually).** Kael–Severin now has a private channel (the walk down): the rehearsal (teaching concealment as curriculum — the family trade in a third teacher's hands, never labeled), question-doors, the one-inch doctrine tell ("Hold that. It is the only thing I am worried you'll lose."). Severin's register: doors with nothing written on them; kindness in valve-form (the fish-and-mutton line — an exit given without acknowledging a door). Grow by inches across Part II; the valve family (liar's knot, winter-feast fact) never tips.
- **DO NOT SOFTEN THE HARSHNESS (counterweight).** The parents are not good parents. Their teaching is at times result-oriented to the point of genuine, reader-discomforting harshness — procedural, calm, total; never rage, never cruelty for its own sake. Kael normalizes and defends it on the page (he is proudest when the harshness "works"), which makes it worse, not better. The love is real AND the harm is real; neither cancels the other. Occasionally a parent glimpses what they are doing — and does not change. They rebuilt the shape of the thing that unmade them. If every harsh scene resolves into reassurance, revision flag. (Ch. 5 instance: "She never noticed that he stopped" — the harm running the other direction now; the concealment they built arriving in him, aimed at them.)
- **MODEL-TIC SELF-AWARENESS (author-ratified 2026-08-20).** The prose is drafted with Claude, and Claude has default rhythms it falls into when not watching itself: standalone em-dashes, "not X but Y" constructions, sentence fragments deployed for gravity, triadic lists. None are banned — the book's voice already uses all of them — but every instance must be a CHOICE, not a default. While drafting, Claude self-flags passages where its own patterns are steering the sentence instead of the voice; revision passes report per-chapter tic counts (dashes, not-buts, fragments, triads — and, added session 12: AND-CHAINS ["and x and y because z" accumulations; and/1k as the gauge] and SIMILE DENSITY ["the way…"/"like" per 1k]) alongside the word count. The two session-12 additions are AWARENESS instruments, not thresholds (author ruling): chain-cadence and simile-dense passages are legitimate where chosen — the audit found them steering by default (37–58 and/1k, ~1 simile per 100 words, everywhere). Upward drift without intent = revision flag. The test is always: would this sentence survive being read aloud in the author's voice, or does it sound like a model being writerly?
- **MAGIC ON THE PAGE (Part I ceiling):** perception-first, non-lexical, craft register only. No mechanics, no terms ("the minima" arrives at Temple enrollment, Part II). Every magic lesson is double work — teaching AND leashing (00, Kynemir); the relief-tell is now SPENT — do not respend.

### 07-Story-Ledger/magic-snapshot.md — VERBATIM pre-rewrite text
## Magic System (canon snapshot — full deep layer now in 00; public layer in 02)
**LAW CORE.** Canon snapshot; deep layer in 00, public layer in 02.

- Six elements (fire, air, water, earth, lightning, nature) — the KNOWN WORLD'S model. One dominant proficiency per Resonant (rare 2–3); capacity and control separate axes. Bloodline-magic doctrine = imperial LIE enforced by pedagogy monopoly. Element DECLARES with maturity; early magic undifferentiated. Kael: lightning proficiency, even spread, LOW capacity, exceptional control.
- **DEEP LAYER (sealed 2026-08-09 — see 00 in full):** matter carries a second order of particle; magic = INFECTION (own particles seeding ambient ones → transmuting matter's nature); elements = taxonomy of common OUTCOMES, not kinds of magic; attunement trainable, almost never self-discovers (mechanical truth of the monopoly); capacity = personal reserve; control = economy of infection ("everything heavy is lazy" = literal law); DRAIN: effort → pain → permanent WHITE HAIR proportional to depth → death at zero. Two body-marks of magic: red eyes (Kynemir) + white hair (drain). KYNEMIR = MOTION, not transmutation — moves particles rather than changing them; no medium; breaks the taxonomy. Different particle vs. different infection: open, far-continent braid.
- **Naming:** empire/Temple scholarly term = **"the minima"** (on-page from Part II). "Magicule" RELEASED (Slime collision). "Vessence" banked (propaganda flourish only). Part I stays non-lexical; Neris's register stays felt-only.
- THE bloodline = KYNEMIR (sealed, 00): kinetic; eyes RED at unleashing; Ch. 4 knot = first raw unleashing, POV-silent. True unleashing reserved (fire = leading candidate). Name never on-page in Book One.


## SESSION 18 ADDENDUM 8 — 2026-08-21 (THE 06/07 BOUNDARY, author-flagged: "standing-rules from 07 kinda sound like style stuff")
- **Boundary principle established: 06 owns the RULE (invariant — how prose is written); 07 owns the STATE (what has been spent, what can no longer be contradicted).** The system already contained the model, correctly applied in one place and nowhere else: 06 Hard Rules holds "the parents are never seen at true capacity until the fire" (the rule) while 07 holds the FATHER-GLIMPSE LEDGER enumerating the three spent glimpses (the state). Every other rule/state pair was filed by topic instead.
- **07 → 06 (5 rules, no story content):** love-is-embedded, DO NOT SOFTEN THE HARSHNESS, SCIENCE REGISTER gradient, MODEL-TIC SELF-AWARENESS, MAGIC ON THE PAGE (Part I ceiling). Their state tails stayed in 07 (the relief-tell spend).
- **06 → 07 (spend state):** the executed pan-away instance (~110w describing Ch. 12's scene) and the two executed first-person slips (~180w) collapse into a DEPARTURE LEDGER in 07, mirroring the father-glimpse ledger. 06 keeps the rules governing them. This state was previously carried at three different lengths in 06, 05 ARC STATE, and implicitly 07 — the classic drift setup.
- **06 defects fixed:** the `Author taste notes (not rules, calibration)` header contained four items explicitly labelled author-ratified RULES (one even said "a rule, kept here for adjacency") — a rule filed under "not rules" is a rule that gets skipped; they were promoted into a proper section and only genuine calibration left behind. Naming Conventions deduped to 02. Status chain and all session stamps removed.
- **Deliberately NOT compressed:** the WHAT/WHY split and KAEL'S DEFINING AXIS counterweight are long because their qualifications ARE the rule. A verbosity pass on 06 is the author's next scheduled item; this pass was structural only.

### 06-Style-Sheet.md — VERBATIM pre-boundary-pass text
# 06 — Style Sheet
**Status:** Mostly stable. Last updated: 2026-08-21, session 15 (pan-away first instance spent — Ch. 12; parents-at-capacity rule amended: braid-school demonstrations licensed, "try" re-scoped to contest/effort). Prior: 2026-08-20, session 12 (POV amended to governed flexibility — pan-aways, interludes, first-person slips; relationship-time + dialogue-mix rules; Kael defining-axis + blooming). Prior: 2026-08-09.

## POV (AMENDED session 12 — hard single-POV rule replaced by governed flexibility, author-ratified)
- **Default (the vast majority of every book):** close third limited, single POV (Kael). Within Kael-scenes the strict information rule is UNCHANGED: the reader never knows more than he does, except by inference.
- **Sanctioned departures** — each rationed, each argued per-use like a heavy-canon beat, none before Part III of Book One unless argued:
  1. **EXTERNAL-CAMERA PAN-AWAYS:** short scenes without Kael — dialogue and observable action ONLY; NO interiority for secret-holding heads (Severin, Vask, Neris, the Gardener — entering them detonates live valves). The cut-before-the-answer is the device (the reader overhears until the cliffhanger, never past it). Native seal available: characters shifting into the downhill language locks the reader out in-world. Departures show machinations MOVING, never sealed answers — they relocate dread; 00 stays sealed by camera placement as much as by silence. **FIRST INSTANCE SPENT (Ch. 12 close, shape ratified session 14, executed session 15):** Severin at the mountain house after the boy sleeps — adult-cold register from the first line ("The boy was asleep when the three of them finally spoke"), dialogue + observables only, the downhill seal used (her three sentences, his one-word reply — reader locked out in-world), cut-before-the-answer on the "how he goes" question; probe confirmed no sealed leakage. The device remains rationed; each next use argued per-use.
  2. **INTERLUDES / SPLIT-GROUP BITS (post-island acts):** third limited in non-secret-holding heads (Valeria the safest); adult-cold register from the first line (contrast with Kael's maturing voice is a feature). Character flashbacks allowed for OTHER characters — THE PARENTS' BACKSTORY EXCEPTION STANDS (below): their past stays archaeology, never flashback.
  3. **FIRST-PERSON REFLECTIVE SLIPS (Kael only):** rare retrospective "I" surfacing at critical emotional turning points — sentences, not scenes. May carry future FEELING and JUDGMENT, never future FACTS or events. Implies a telling-frame (told to whom, from when — braids toward the witness structure); the frame stays DELIBERATELY UNRESOLVED — do not commit without a dedicated session. **FIRST INSTANCE SPENT (argued and author-ratified, session 13): the Ch. 1 micro-overture** — five sentences of future feeling/judgment ("I was happy...") ahead of "Before anything else, there was the fire," which now works as the hinge into the child's head. Zero future facts; frame unresolved (no addressee, no when). Retroactively legitimizes Ch. 6's "later, much later" line as the same voice surfacing. Deliberate echo logged: "both were true" runs adjacent to "both things were real" (the family trade, in the future voice). **SLIP #2 SPENT (Ch. 13 close, author-directed session 16 — the between-slip argued and ratified in-session):** the coat block ("the wearer pays for it... both things were true. Both things were always true.") — the overture's dark sibling; zero future facts ("heavier coats since" = feeling/judgment). THE TRIPTYCH RATIFIED: happiness claimed (Ch. 1) → cost named (Ch. 13) → the voice's return at THE FIRE (slip 3 — now firmly reserved; no slip between Ch. 13 and the fire without a dedicated argument). Frame still deliberately unresolved. **TYPOGRAPHIC CONVENTION (ratified session 13): all first-person slips are set in italics** — block-italic for overtures, italic sentences when a slip surfaces mid-scene; distinct from word-level emphasis italics, which continue unchanged.

## The Maturing Voice
- Early childhood: short sentences, concrete senses, literal child-logic. The village lights are "the stars that fell down the mountain."
- Middle childhood: questions sharpen; sentences lengthen; pattern-noticing.
- Adolescence+: complexity, irony, self-awareness.

## Dread by Inference
- Menace is shown through what a child half-notices; the reader assembles it. Never explain.
- Assassin-craft is presented as normal chores/lessons — wrongness exists only in the reader's head.

## Hard Rules
- **The parents' backstory is never told, only inherited.** No flashbacks, no confession scenes. Kael learns who they were like an archaeologist: objects, scars, reflexes, other people's fear — across the whole series. (SURVIVES the session-12 POV amendment: pan-aways may show the parents in the PRESENT, external camera only; their PAST stays archaeology — this rule outranks the flashback allowance for other characters.)
- **The parents are never seen at true capacity until the fire.** Training scenes are constant and welcome — sparring, drills, corrections — but they always operate at Kael's ceiling, never their own (concealment is their resting state, even at home). Glimpses only: a strike blocked without looking up; one blur of motion the mother seems embarrassed by. Kael never beats either of them, and eventually realizes he has never once seen them *try* — a beat to land mid-book, once the Temple gives him a reference class. The fire answers the question once, completely. **AMENDED session 15 (the braid school):** from Ch. 12 on, DELIBERATE TEACHING DEMONSTRATIONS at demonstration scale are licensed (first instance: Vask's lightning-threaded hand through the trunk — calm, precision-framed, no effort shown; Kael reads it as exactness, not power). The rule's core is re-scoped, not weakened: Kael has still never seen either parent in CONTEST or at EFFORT — "never seen them try" means *tried*, not *demonstrated* — and the reflex-glimpse ledger (07) is untouched by demonstrations. The fire remains the only full answer.
- **Kael's social blindness is shown, not diagnosed.** He misreads on the page; the narration never labels it.
- **THE WHAT/WHY SPLIT (author-ratified session 13 — governs Kael's people-reading for the series).** His physical reading is extraordinary — ten years of training; his social reading is near-nonexistent — and the gap must NOT close quickly. Render the split explicitly: he may correctly detect the WHAT (he's angry; she's lying; the room turned) while completely misreading the WHY (the motive; whether a lie is cruel, protective, embarrassed, playful, or kind). Let him make real mistakes, on the page, uncorrected — the loom-glance ("they are checking I did it correctly") is the model. The long development is series-scale, and it is an ARC, not a fixed trait (author clarification, session 13): he eventually discovers that people are not patterns to solve — they have intentions, contradictions, private histories, and the ability to deliberately change the pattern — and past that discovery his social reading grows into EXTREME PROFICIENCY: the pattern-mind, once it accepts what people are, turns fully onto them (the Requiem is, structurally, the endpoint's proof — engineering how a whole world will read him). The rule constrains PACE, not destination: no deterministic "he can never read people"; competence is earned late, through real mistakes, and arrives fully.
- **Fights are puzzles.** Kael wins by legible technique and decision-making, never by power-ups. The reader should understand every victory.

## Genre Register
- Epic, gritty, historical-flavored fantasy. Intimate over spectacular. Earned over destined.

## Naming Conventions
- Three registers (see World Bible): village-plain / imperial-ornate / guard-hard. Names carry sociology; commoners lack surnames.

## Things to Avoid
- Info-dumps; POV breaks; telling the reader what to feel; characters as plot functions (Character Law); hidden-puppet-master vibes for the leader — he stays human, limited, doctrine-driven.

## Author taste notes (session 8 — not rules, calibration)
- **Interiority is the seasoning, scene is the meal.** Kael's deep head is the story's depth — keep it — but favor dialogue and real-time action over narrated summary whenever both can carry the beat. A LOT of descriptive/interior text over action and dialogue reads boring to the author.
- **The causal chain stops at the first why.** The child's-logic chains earned their density in Ch. 1–3; from Part II, links that re-derive established canon ("the why of the why") get cut — trust the plant. Not a flag system; an ear.
- **KAEL'S DEFINING AXIS (author-ratified session 11 — a rule, kept here for adjacency).** His defining features are intelligence, technique, and practical knowledge of the field — hunting, growing, weather, terrain, the body, the sciences of his mother's schools — AND exceptional fighting: martial skill, balance, physical prowess, hand-to-hand and weapons, all of it his parents' teaching in him. The deficiency is ONE axis only: keep a HEAVY, persistent emphasis on his lack of magic capacity — and on the empire/Temple wrongly over-emphasizing capacity as the measure of a Resonant. The world keeps saying the loud wrong thing about him; the page keeps showing the quiet true thing. Neither half is allowed to fade. And as magic grows more and more important across the series, his TECHNIQUE AND CONTROL remain his greatest asset — the axis the capacity-worshipping world undervalues is the axis that wins; every escalation of magic's stakes re-proves it. **COUNTERWEIGHT (author-ratified session 13): the critique is of capacity-WORSHIP as the sole measure — never of capacity, mass, leadership, or social gravity themselves.** Those are real powers, valued by the story and, on the page, by Kael's own honest pricing (Aurelian's count was "well given" and Kael filed it): command moves six backs where the pry-bar moves none, the empire's armies run on it, and Kael's lack of it is a REAL deficit, not a hidden virtue. Render the complementarity — perhaps equal in weight to his economy, timing, perception, and leverage; never hand-wave the other column away.
- **THE BLOOMING (author-ratified session 11).** From Part II on, his wits and intelligence bloom visibly as he grows — quicker in dialogue, drier and faster in thought, pattern-jumps arriving sooner. The maturing-voice gradient explicitly includes intelligence made audible in speech and interiority, not just sentence complexity.
- **RELATIONSHIP TIME IS LOAD-BEARING (author-ratified session 12).** Every chapter carries at least one scene whose only job is a relationship moving — warmth, friction, play, pride — not plot, not clue-work. Rendered in the established registers (tells, shared work, jokes, the body), but allowed to BREATHE: real dialogue, real laughter that isn't threat. The never-stated rule holds; the fix is TIME, not sentiment. The Oss cooling and the fire only devastate in proportion to the warmth banked beforehand.
- **DIALOGUE AND EXPOSITION MIX (author direction, session 12).** The manuscript runs dialogue-starved (audit: 0–14% by chapter against fiction's typical 25–50%). Exposition and interiority must be mixed and matched with spoken scene — lessons in the teacher's actual voice, conflicts in the room's actual words. Not a quota; an ear. Emotional conflict belongs on the surface of scenes as well as under them.
- **The supporting cast shines.** Every significant character gets moments that are THEIRS — voice, body, presence — good or bad; Kael is sometimes the camera, not the subject. Show, don't tell (some tell allowed). Physical descriptions come from 03's APPEARANCE LEDGER, filtered through Kael's eye; others' reactions carry conventional beauty.

### 07-Story-Ledger/standing-rules.md — VERBATIM pre-boundary-pass text
## Standing Rules (from author)
**LAW CORE.** Author-ratified rules that govern how the prose is written.

- **Love is embedded in the teaching, never stated.** Every stern lesson must carry the signature of protection in its stitching (the hand before the ground; the lie that makes a horizon). If a teaching scene reads stern without detectable love, revision flag.
- **FATHER-GLIMPSE LEDGER (ration hard).** Sanctioned glimpses of Vask's true capacity spent: (1) the slate, Ch. 1; (2) rising out of the nothing, Ch. 2; (3) the wrist catch, Ch. 3. Ch. 4 and Ch. 5: zero spent. EXCEPTION EXECUTED (Ch. 6): the rescue rendered ONLY as fragments through half-conscious POV (sound stopped mid-note; absence; hands; "a quiet the size of several men") — Kael could never testify to what happened; no comparison frame built (deliberately: the reader never sees Vask do what Kael couldn't). The account holds at three-plus-fragments and is now VERY expensive. No new glimpses until the "never seen them try" beat (mid-book). The fire remains the only full answer.
- **THE KYNEMIR ABSORBED-STATE RULE (sealed in 00 — page-facing consequence):** the blood surfaces from the absorbed/flow state, NEVER from panic or fear. Ch. 6's beating therefore correctly shows no surfacing. Any future scene of Kael in mortal terror must NOT flicker; any future absorbed-state scene is live territory. The fire candidate depends on him being past panic, into something else.
- **GROUP LAUGHTER IS NOW A CHARGED REGISTER FOR KAEL (Ch. 6 onward, unmarked).** Every future scene where people laugh around him — the Temple yards, feasts, the golden children — has the settlement under it. Never labeled; render as his small physical tells only. Its term of art on the page: **"a crop of laughter"** (a deliberate coinage — logged in 02's lexicon; four instances Ch. 8–10; further uses deliberate-only). The register's origin runs the full night of Ch. 6 in three strikes (the spotting, their own man down, "someone was laughing still").
- **SCIENCE REGISTER — A GRADIENT (full rule in 02).** The permanent ban is on OUT-OF-WORLD vocabulary only (no modern terms, no formulas as notation). Knowledge, method, and precision deepen WITHOUT CEILING across the series. Part I = craft-lore (holds as written). Part II+ = the HONESTY PIVOT (canonized Ch. 7): the parents teach with precision where they can afford it; Kael's language matures into genuine period natural philosophy; he coins his own terms where the world has none (far-counting precedent — the lexicon habit is a canonized trait, see 03); the EXPERIMENTAL TURN begins (his own trials — "asking twice with one thing changed"). Sealed irony (00): honesty in five schools, rationing in three (their past, history, magic) — the pivot draws the map of their secrets in negative space; Kael will notice the asymmetry (filed unknowing Ch. 7 — Part II beat). If a lesson reads MODERN, revision flag; if it reads merely precise, that is now correct.
- **THREE STACKED SYSTEMS (Part II engine).** Kael arrives at the Temple carrying three systems at once: ignorance + settlement fear + armor; the golden children read the composite as strangeness. His threat-calibration (set in the yard) makes their worst fail to register as danger; his calm under mockery enrages; escalation spiral. Runs unlabeled under every Temple-yard scene.
- **CRACKED WASTER — INERT FOREVER.** The waster with the seated crack (Ch. 9, Lucan's rig) is spent: the crack-up return WAS the resolution. Do not bring it back as a device.
- **THE WEAPONS SCHOOL (opened Ch. 7).** Sword, spear, bow — on WASTERS (Vask-carved ash; steel is banked — the Temple's, or the fire's). Runs at home in parallel with the Temple years (mornings stay Vask's), powering the concealment thread from both ends: the Temple never sees what the mountain teaches; the mountain never asks what he shows the Temple. Vask's Ch. 2 register holds (beside him, longer, better; corrections one stroke). At-capacity rule untouched.
- **THE MENTOR THREAD (opened Ch. 7 — develop gradually).** Kael–Severin now has a private channel (the walk down): the rehearsal (teaching concealment as curriculum — the family trade in a third teacher's hands, never labeled), question-doors, the one-inch doctrine tell ("Hold that. It is the only thing I am worried you'll lose."). Severin's register: doors with nothing written on them; kindness in valve-form (the fish-and-mutton line — an exit given without acknowledging a door). Grow by inches across Part II; the valve family (liar's knot, winter-feast fact) never tips.
- **DO NOT SOFTEN THE HARSHNESS (counterweight).** The parents are not good parents. Their teaching is at times result-oriented to the point of genuine, reader-discomforting harshness — procedural, calm, total; never rage, never cruelty for its own sake. Kael normalizes and defends it on the page (he is proudest when the harshness "works"), which makes it worse, not better. The love is real AND the harm is real; neither cancels the other. Occasionally a parent glimpses what they are doing — and does not change. They rebuilt the shape of the thing that unmade them. If every harsh scene resolves into reassurance, revision flag. (Ch. 5 instance: "She never noticed that he stopped" — the harm running the other direction now; the concealment they built arriving in him, aimed at them.)
- **MODEL-TIC SELF-AWARENESS.** The prose is drafted with Claude, and Claude has default rhythms it falls into when not watching itself: standalone em-dashes, "not X but Y" constructions, sentence fragments deployed for gravity, triadic lists. None are banned — the book's voice already uses all of them — but every instance must be a CHOICE, not a default. While drafting, Claude self-flags passages where its own patterns are steering the sentence instead of the voice; revision passes report per-chapter tic counts alongside the word count: dashes, not-buts, fragments, triads, AND-CHAINS ("and x and y because z" accumulations; and/1k as the gauge), SIMILE DENSITY ("the way…"/"like" per 1k). AND-chains and simile density are AWARENESS instruments, not thresholds (author ruling): both are legitimate where chosen. Upward drift without intent = revision flag. The test is always: would this sentence survive being read aloud in the author's voice, or does it sound like a model being writerly?
- **MAGIC ON THE PAGE (Part I ceiling):** perception-first, non-lexical, craft register only. No mechanics, no terms ("the minima" arrives at Temple enrollment, Part II). Every magic lesson is double work — teaching AND leashing (00, Kynemir); the relief-tell is now SPENT — do not respend.


## SESSION 18 ADDENDUM 9 — 2026-08-21 (06 VERBOSITY CUT, author-approved from a proposed cut table)

Per-bullet cut table proposed and approved before editing. Protected and untouched: every qualification that changes what gets written — the WHAT/WHY arc-not-trait clause and its PACE-not-destination limit; the defining axis COUNTERWEIGHT in full; the never-seen-them-try re-scoping; slips carry FEELING/JUDGMENT never FACTS; no interiority for secret-holding heads; the parents-backstory rule outranking the flashback allowance; the read-aloud test; the loom-glance model. Aurelian's "well given" example KEPT inline by author decision — it is recorded nowhere outside the manuscript and this log, and it is the clearest illustration of the counterweight.

### 06-Style-Sheet.md — VERBATIM pre-cut text
# 06 — Style Sheet
**Status:** THE RULE SIDE — how the prose is written; invariant. Spend state (which departures are used, which glimpses spent) is the STATE side: `07-Story-Ledger/standing-rules.md`, with the running count in 05 §ARC STATE. Vocabulary and naming registers: `02-World-Bible/lexicon.md` and `naming.md`. History: `archive/session-log.md`.

## POV
- **Default (the vast majority of every book):** close third limited, single POV (Kael). Within Kael-scenes the strict information rule is UNCHANGED: the reader never knows more than he does, except by inference.
- **Sanctioned departures** — each rationed, each argued per-use like a heavy-canon beat, none before Part III of Book One unless argued:
  1. **EXTERNAL-CAMERA PAN-AWAYS:** short scenes without Kael — dialogue and observable action ONLY; NO interiority for secret-holding heads (Severin, Vask, Neris, the Gardener — entering them detonates live valves). The cut-before-the-answer is the device (the reader overhears until the cliffhanger, never past it). Native seal available: characters shifting into the downhill language locks the reader out in-world. Departures show machinations MOVING, never sealed answers — they relocate dread; 00 stays sealed by camera placement as much as by silence.
  2. **INTERLUDES / SPLIT-GROUP BITS (post-island acts):** third limited in non-secret-holding heads (Valeria the safest); adult-cold register from the first line (contrast with Kael's maturing voice is a feature). Character flashbacks allowed for OTHER characters — THE PARENTS' BACKSTORY EXCEPTION STANDS (below): their past stays archaeology, never flashback.
  3. **FIRST-PERSON REFLECTIVE SLIPS (Kael only):** rare retrospective "I" surfacing at critical emotional turning points — sentences, not scenes. May carry future FEELING and JUDGMENT, never future FACTS or events. Implies a telling-frame (told to whom, from when — braids toward the witness structure); the frame stays DELIBERATELY UNRESOLVED — do not commit without a dedicated session. **TYPOGRAPHIC CONVENTION: all first-person slips are set in italics** — block-italic for overtures, italic sentences when a slip surfaces mid-scene; distinct from word-level emphasis italics, which continue unchanged.
- **Which instances are spent and what remains reserved: the DEPARTURE LEDGER in `07-Story-Ledger/standing-rules.md`.**

## The Maturing Voice
- Early childhood: short sentences, concrete senses, literal child-logic. The village lights are "the stars that fell down the mountain."
- Middle childhood: questions sharpen; sentences lengthen; pattern-noticing.
- Adolescence+: complexity, irony, self-awareness.
- **THE BLOOMING.** From Part II on, his wits and intelligence bloom visibly as he grows — quicker in dialogue, drier and faster in thought, pattern-jumps arriving sooner. The gradient explicitly includes intelligence made audible in speech and interiority, not just sentence complexity.

## Dread by Inference
- Menace is shown through what a child half-notices; the reader assembles it. Never explain.
- Assassin-craft is presented as normal chores/lessons — wrongness exists only in the reader's head.

## Hard Rules
- **The parents' backstory is never told, only inherited.** No flashbacks, no confession scenes. Kael learns who they were like an archaeologist: objects, scars, reflexes, other people's fear — across the whole series. (SURVIVES the POV amendment: pan-aways may show the parents in the PRESENT, external camera only; their PAST stays archaeology — this rule outranks the flashback allowance for other characters.)
- **The parents are never seen at true capacity until the fire.** Training scenes are constant and welcome — sparring, drills, corrections — but they always operate at Kael's ceiling, never their own (concealment is their resting state, even at home). Glimpses only: a strike blocked without looking up; one blur of motion the mother seems embarrassed by. Kael never beats either of them, and eventually realizes he has never once seen them *try* — a beat to land mid-book, once the Temple gives him a reference class. The fire answers the question once, completely. **AMENDED (the braid school):** from Ch. 12 on, DELIBERATE TEACHING DEMONSTRATIONS at demonstration scale are licensed (first instance: Vask's lightning-threaded hand through the trunk — calm, precision-framed, no effort shown; Kael reads it as exactness, not power). The rule's core is re-scoped, not weakened: Kael has still never seen either parent in CONTEST or at EFFORT — "never seen them try" means *tried*, not *demonstrated* — and the reflex-glimpse ledger (07) is untouched by demonstrations. The fire remains the only full answer.
- **Love is embedded in the teaching, never stated.** Every stern lesson must carry the signature of protection in its stitching (the hand before the ground; the lie that makes a horizon). If a teaching scene reads stern without detectable love, revision flag.
- **DO NOT SOFTEN THE HARSHNESS (counterweight).** The parents are not good parents. Their teaching is at times result-oriented to the point of genuine, reader-discomforting harshness — procedural, calm, total; never rage, never cruelty for its own sake. Kael normalizes and defends it on the page (he is proudest when the harshness "works"), which makes it worse, not better. The love is real AND the harm is real; neither cancels the other. Occasionally a parent glimpses what they are doing — and does not change. They rebuilt the shape of the thing that unmade them. If every harsh scene resolves into reassurance, revision flag. (Ch. 5 instance: "She never noticed that he stopped" — the harm running the other direction now; the concealment they built arriving in him, aimed at them.)
- **Kael's social blindness is shown, not diagnosed.** He misreads on the page; the narration never labels it.
- **THE WHAT/WHY SPLIT (governs Kael's people-reading for the series).** His physical reading is extraordinary — ten years of training; his social reading is near-nonexistent — and the gap must NOT close quickly. Render the split explicitly: he may correctly detect the WHAT (he's angry; she's lying; the room turned) while completely misreading the WHY (the motive; whether a lie is cruel, protective, embarrassed, playful, or kind). Let him make real mistakes, on the page, uncorrected — the loom-glance ("they are checking I did it correctly") is the model. The long development is series-scale, and it is an ARC, not a fixed trait: he eventually discovers that people are not patterns to solve — they have intentions, contradictions, private histories, and the ability to deliberately change the pattern — and past that discovery his social reading grows into EXTREME PROFICIENCY: the pattern-mind, once it accepts what people are, turns fully onto them (the Requiem is, structurally, the endpoint's proof — engineering how a whole world will read him). The rule constrains PACE, not destination: no deterministic "he can never read people"; competence is earned late, through real mistakes, and arrives fully.
- **Fights are puzzles.** Kael wins by legible technique and decision-making, never by power-ups. The reader should understand every victory.

## Register
- **SCIENCE REGISTER — A GRADIENT (full rule in 02).** The permanent ban is on OUT-OF-WORLD vocabulary only (no modern terms, no formulas as notation). Knowledge, method, and precision deepen WITHOUT CEILING across the series. Part I = craft-lore (holds as written). Part II+ = the HONESTY PIVOT (canonized Ch. 7): the parents teach with precision where they can afford it; Kael's language matures into genuine period natural philosophy; he coins his own terms where the world has none (far-counting precedent — the lexicon habit is a canonized trait, see 03); the EXPERIMENTAL TURN begins (his own trials — "asking twice with one thing changed"). Sealed irony (00): honesty in five schools, rationing in three (their past, history, magic) — the pivot draws the map of their secrets in negative space. If a lesson reads MODERN, revision flag; if it reads merely precise, that is now correct.
- **MAGIC ON THE PAGE (Part I ceiling):** perception-first, non-lexical, craft register only. No mechanics, no terms ("the minima" arrives at Temple enrollment, Part II). Every magic lesson is double work — teaching AND leashing (00, Kynemir).
- **Naming:** three registers — village-plain / imperial-ornate / guard-hard. Names carry sociology; commoners lack surnames. Full conventions in `02-World-Bible/naming.md`; blocked and available names in `07-Story-Ledger/established-facts.md`.

## Character & Scene Rules
- **KAEL'S DEFINING AXIS.** His defining features are intelligence, technique, and practical knowledge of the field — hunting, growing, weather, terrain, the body, the sciences of his mother's schools — AND exceptional fighting: martial skill, balance, physical prowess, hand-to-hand and weapons, all of it his parents' teaching in him. The deficiency is ONE axis only: keep a HEAVY, persistent emphasis on his lack of magic capacity — and on the empire/Temple wrongly over-emphasizing capacity as the measure of a Resonant. The world keeps saying the loud wrong thing about him; the page keeps showing the quiet true thing. Neither half is allowed to fade. And as magic grows more and more important across the series, his TECHNIQUE AND CONTROL remain his greatest asset — the axis the capacity-worshipping world undervalues is the axis that wins; every escalation of magic's stakes re-proves it. **COUNTERWEIGHT: the critique is of capacity-WORSHIP as the sole measure — never of capacity, mass, leadership, or social gravity themselves.** Those are real powers, valued by the story and, on the page, by Kael's own honest pricing (Aurelian's count was "well given" and Kael filed it): command moves six backs where the pry-bar moves none, the empire's armies run on it, and Kael's lack of it is a REAL deficit, not a hidden virtue. Render the complementarity — perhaps equal in weight to his economy, timing, perception, and leverage; never hand-wave the other column away.
- **RELATIONSHIP TIME IS LOAD-BEARING.** Every chapter carries at least one scene whose only job is a relationship moving — warmth, friction, play, pride — not plot, not clue-work. Rendered in the established registers (tells, shared work, jokes, the body), but allowed to BREATHE: real dialogue, real laughter that isn't threat. The never-stated rule holds; the fix is TIME, not sentiment. The Oss cooling and the fire only devastate in proportion to the warmth banked beforehand.
- **DIALOGUE AND EXPOSITION MIX.** The manuscript runs dialogue-starved (audit: 0–14% by chapter against fiction's typical 25–50%). Exposition and interiority must be mixed and matched with spoken scene — lessons in the teacher's actual voice, conflicts in the room's actual words. Not a quota; an ear. Emotional conflict belongs on the surface of scenes as well as under them.
- **The supporting cast shines.** Every significant character gets moments that are THEIRS — voice, body, presence — good or bad; Kael is sometimes the camera, not the subject. Show, don't tell (some tell allowed). Physical descriptions come from 03's APPEARANCE LEDGER, filtered through Kael's eye; others' reactions carry conventional beauty.

## Model-Tic Self-Awareness
- The prose is drafted with Claude, and Claude has default rhythms it falls into when not watching itself: standalone em-dashes, "not X but Y" constructions, sentence fragments deployed for gravity, triadic lists. None are banned — the book's voice already uses all of them — but every instance must be a CHOICE, not a default. While drafting, Claude self-flags passages where its own patterns are steering the sentence instead of the voice; revision passes report per-chapter tic counts alongside the word count: dashes, not-buts, fragments, triads, AND-CHAINS ("and x and y because z" accumulations; and/1k as the gauge), SIMILE DENSITY ("the way…"/"like" per 1k). AND-chains and simile density are AWARENESS instruments, not thresholds (author ruling): both are legitimate where chosen. Upward drift without intent = revision flag. The test is always: would this sentence survive being read aloud in the author's voice, or does it sound like a model being writerly?

## Genre Register
- Epic, gritty, historical-flavored fantasy. Intimate over spectacular. Earned over destined.

## Things to Avoid
- Info-dumps; POV breaks; telling the reader what to feel; characters as plot functions (Character Law); hidden-puppet-master vibes for the leader — he stays human, limited, doctrine-driven.

## Author taste notes (calibration, not rules)
- **Interiority is the seasoning, scene is the meal.** Kael's deep head is the story's depth — keep it — but favor dialogue and real-time action over narrated summary whenever both can carry the beat. A LOT of descriptive/interior text over action and dialogue reads boring to the author.
- **The causal chain stops at the first why.** The child's-logic chains earned their density in Ch. 1–3; from Part II, links that re-derive established canon ("the why of the why") get cut — trust the plant. Not a flag system; an ear.

### CLAUDE.md — VERBATIM pre-review text
# Novel Project — Working Protocol (Claude Code era)

This is a fiction project. The document system is the sole source of truth; chat history is disposable. Migrated from Claude Chat 2026-08-20; the repo is now a git repository and git history is the authority on what changed.

## Files
Every file has a CONTRACT (what it holds), an ADMISSION TEST, and a named EVICTION PATH. Content that fails its file's test moves; it does not accumulate in place.

- `00-Author-Canon-SEALED.md` = SEALED author canon (truths of the story that may NOT yet be revealed on the page. Never contradict; reveal slowly. Append-only, dated blocks; never edit without author ratification.)
- `01`, `06` = bibles (premise, style).
- `02-World-Bible/`, `03-Character-Bible/` = FOLDERS since session 18 (02 — topic files; 03 — one file per character + `appearance-ledger.md`). Each has `index.md` = pure TOC, no facts, and `changelog.md` = one line per session. "Read 02/03" = read every file in the folder.
- `04-Outline.md` = THE FORWARD PLAN. Test: *is this not yet written?* Spent beats keep a one-line pin only (the numbers are load-bearing addresses cited from 00/02/03/05/07). EVICTION: at each chapter commit the beat collapses to its pin and its text rolls to `archive/outline-archive.md`. Unspent material with no fixed beat lives in §Queued threads.
- `05-Continuity-Log.md` = LIVE STATE. Test: *will this be false in ten sessions?* If it will still be true, it belongs elsewhere. EVICTION: status entries are pointer-style and the oldest rolls to the archive at each close; tic reports go straight to the archive.
- `07-Story-Ledger/` = LAW (do-not-contradict canon), a FOLDER since session 18, split on GROWTH BEHAVIOUR. `index.md` = pure TOC. LAW CORE (timeline, standing-rules, planted-clues, magic-snapshot, open-threads, established-facts) is stable — read in full every session. ACCUMULATORS (chapter-records, protected-phrasings) grow every chapter. Test: *could a future page contradict this?* EVICTION: per-book roll — at each book boundary the accumulators roll to `*-book-N.md` and leave the every-session read; the law core never moves.
- `archive/session-log.md` = HISTORY (append-only session records and design logs — spot-read on demand, never edited)
- `archive/outline-archive.md` = 04's spent-beat text and superseded plans — spot-read on demand
- `manuscript/book-one/NN-Title.md` = manuscript chapters

## Session protocol
Folded in from 05 §Working Protocol at session-18 addendum 6; verbatim pre-fold text and each rule's session provenance are in the archive. These rules win.

**Open**
1. VERIFY: run `scripts/verify.sh` (git tree clean + `wc -w` vs the CANONICAL baselines in 05). Anything modified or untracked that no log entry explains = STOP and report before reading further.
2. COLD READ, then flag anything that itches BEFORE any work — treat it as a free continuity audit. **TIERED COLD READ is in effect** (called session 15, from session 16 on):
   - all bibles in full, every session, forever — they are the compressed truth and must stay fully loaded;
   - the most recent 2–3 chapters in full, non-negotiable — the maturing-voice gradient is calibrated locally, chapter against neighbours; no summary carries cadence;
   - older chapters via `07-Story-Ledger/chapter-records.md`, plus targeted spot-reads of anything the session will touch (an echo, a protected phrasing, a scene being rhymed against). Skip a chapter's record when reading that chapter in full — the record substitutes for the chapter, it does not double it.
   - The archive is history, not part of the cold read — spot-read it only when a session record is needed. This is safe because continuity lives in the established facts, protected phrasings and clue ledger, never in rereading the manuscript.
3. Confirm target via the NEXT TARGET block in 05. Never improvise a target, only if requested.

**Work**
4. New chapters: mandatory design argument first (as specified in NEXT TARGET) → propose chapter shape → WAIT for approval → draft.
5. Revisions: propose per-chapter cut/addition tables with projected counts; flat-or-leaner rule per chapter (against 05's CANONICAL baselines); failed passes get redone. Run `scripts/check-echoes.sh` on any new/revised chapter (every protected-phrase hit must be a deliberate echo) and report tic counts per the MODEL-TIC SELF-AWARENESS rule (06 §Model-Tic Self-Awareness).
6. Rhythm: open on `git status` clean → draft → react/revise while hot → approve → commit decisions to the files → `git commit` → close. The working files ARE the canon; there is no reupload step.
7. Ceiling: two chapters of new prose per session; three only if structurally simple. Heavy-canon chapters (the tournament, the fire) get a session to themselves, with a planning session beforehand if needed. The ceiling applies to NEW prose only — revision passes and line-edits are cheap.

**Close**
8. Every decision commits to a file before session close. Session closes with a `git commit` (short imperative subject) + `git push`; update baselines in 05 whenever prose changes; re-sync the reader notebook after prose changes.
9. Hygiene at close: append one changelog line to any 02/03/07 folder the session touched; roll the oldest inline 05 status block to the archive; collapse any newly spent 04 beat to its pin.

## Standing working rules
- **NOTHING LIVES IN CHAT HISTORY.** Every decision, rule, or promising unresolved idea gets written into a file before session end. Chat transcripts are disposable scaffolding.
- **VERSION CONTROL IS THE SAFETY NET.** Git history is the authority on what changed; the `wc -w` baselines in 05 remain as a cheap cross-check and as the flat-or-leaner budget. Cut material is recoverable from history — restoration is one edit, so cut freely.
- **ZERO-LOSS DISCIPLINE for doc-system work.** Any restructure moves content VERBATIM, then verifies: every non-empty line of the old file present in the new, coverage of anything dropped, and a parity run of any script that parses the file. Report the verification, don't assert it.

## Instruments
- **Reader-copy notebook (NotebookLM via `nlm`):** clean-reader simulator holding manuscript chapters ONLY — never add bibles, 00, or 05 to it (its blindness to sealed canon is the point). Query it for legibility probes before committing a chapter; treat answers as reader-inference evidence, never canon. Re-sync after prose changes: `scripts/sync-reader-notebook.sh` (part of session close). Full rules in TOOLS.md; probe history in the archive.
- `nlm` has further dormant capabilities (reports, mind maps, audio overviews, expectation probes) — catalog and usage rules in `TOOLS.md`.

## Hard rules
- Never edit 00 without explicit author ratification; additions are dated append-only blocks.
- Commit after every author-ratified change; never leave ratified work uncommitted at session close.
- Historical records (session logs, design logs, the archive) are records — append, don't rewrite.

### Planted-clues audit — PROCESS NOTE (first attempt was defective)
The first normalisation pass silently corrupted all 109 rows: the leading-state-word regex was anchored on an asterisk that the preceding line had already stripped, so every row ended up with its state word duplicated ("**PLANTED** — PLANTED (visit 1"), and a careless `.rstrip(") ")` removed closing parens from 21 rows. Caught by running the defect audit on the output instead of trusting it, then reverted from HEAD and redone. Final verification on the redo: 109 rows in and out; zero unintended changes to the Clue / Planted-in / Pays-off columns (only the two deliberate status corrections); zero handling text lost; echo parity identical. The lesson is the standing rule as written — report the verification, do not assert it; the first pass would have read as successful from its own summary.

3. **THE MEDIUM LAW.** Author ruled lightning should thread metal MORE easily than wood, and that Kael's scientific mind should get ideas from it. Steel takes a thread unasked and carries it whole; the same property returns it, so the burst scales with the road. Yielded THE BIND — Kael's untested hypothesis, written in the blank book, never attempted (02, logged OPEN, not canon until spent).
4. **BRUTALITY SCALED.** Kael's obsession and Vask's fear meeting and each mistaking the other for agreement. Proofing; the night hours Kael asks for; the marks never above the collar or below the wrist (coat-aware harshness); Neris twice in the doorway; Vask's one glimpse.

Vask's briefing on Temple steel also cut — he would not know it and would not say it; the information moved to where Kael would actually meet it, which made the mountain visibly ahead of the empire.
6. **CHAPTER CUT AT THE WATCHING.** The drafted tail (2,079w: her asking for him in the yard, the losing season, the drawer shut deliberately, the judge, the First Master, the gorse catch) was removed at the author's direction for redesign. Diagnosis recorded at the time: the tail failed because the MODE changed — everything before it is scene, the tail is summary, and the rivalry is the one thing that most needs dramatising. It survives only in git b482ced and is NOT a plan. The Temple-steel section was then moved above the cistern court so the chapter closes on the watching, and the burn-count span trimmed to midsummer.

**Ch. 15 result.** 5,479w, five sections. ### BEAT-14 DESIGN ARGUMENT (delivered session 23; DECISIONS OWED — no approval given, nothing drafted)
- **THE TRAP.** The obvious chapter is *Valeria opens up*, and it spends the mask — 03 is explicit: *ration her interiority, keep the mask.* Kael also cannot be the one who unlocks her: the WHAT/WHY split is at its widest with her, and 07 forbids narration correcting the Ch. 15 misread. If he asks the right question, the chapter cheats.
- **THE THESIS: the crack is not a confession, it is a LEAK INSIDE A REFUSAL.** She refuses pity the way she refused cynicism in Ch. 17 — flatly, without understanding the complaint. Kael reaches toward *your school is worse than mine*; she refuses it and defends her father; **the defence is the wound.** Ch. 15's *"Nobody stands beside her"* comes home by being REJECTED by her, never corrected by narration.
- **THE DETONATOR IS THE WEATHER, and 02 already owns the word: the STORM-WEEKS** (the drowned-yard weeks of deep winter, children's vernacular, unglossed). Take the courts and you take away the only social language Kael has: two people whose entire relationship is a fight, in a room, with no fight available. Nothing else in Part III does that. **It also solves the sleeplessness** without the cheap line — she has been first on court by a quarter-hour every day of her life, the storms took the court, and she now has hours with nowhere to put them. Kael counts his way to it as a logistics problem and asks the wrong question about it and gets a true answer by accident.
- **HE HAS TO PAY or the bond goes asymmetric** (she is the designed control-opposite of Oss). He cannot give her the mountain, and she still never asks — that is HERS and must not become leverage. What he can pay is the one true thing that is not the mountain: **the because-shaped hole.** She has nothing but purpose and it is not hers; he has nothing but appetite and it is going nowhere; neither can hand the other anything.
- **THE LAST BEAT IS KAEL GETTING IT WRONG.** 03 banks the microscopic flinch at reverence for her name, unspent. Fire it in private, at something small — her name in a stranger's mouth in a corridor. He reads bodies better than anyone alive and people worse than anyone alive: he sees the WHAT perfectly, misreads the WHY completely, **says it out loud, is wrong, and she lets him be wrong.** The honest night-conversation with no narrated sympathy anywhere in it.
- **PROPOSED SHAPE — one chapter, ~4,800–5,400w, the cold term into the storm-weeks.** I — THE DROWNED YARD: the storm-weeks; the road walked differently and armed; **the war pre-seeds as weather** (Part III owes them and the mountain cannot pay them) — senior-cohort students summoned home, a lesson quietly rewritten, letters arriving strange; Kael sees the pattern and has nowhere to put it. II — FULL BUT HEAVY: the Elarine purpose dialogue on its new trigger *(cut entirely under decision B)*; her coat-wire stays unresolved. III — THE QUARTER-HOUR: no court, their hour has nowhere to go, the sleeplessness reached by arithmetic. IV — THE HONEST HOUR: staged as an ARGUMENT, not a confession; the misread refused; the because-shaped hole paid. V — THE FLINCH: the WHAT read perfectly and the WHY missed out loud; close on the name.
- **STAYING OUT:** the chest · Kynemir · any father-glimpse · the insignia · Severin's valves · Halden (the fuse stays unlit) · the rematch (spent Ch. 17/18) · **her first-exchange lag** — banked, and a chapter that takes the fights away is exactly where it would be spent cheaply · the mountain. **No refusal anywhere — beat 16 is protected and nobody asks Kael for anything.**
- **DECISION 1 (owed): does the Elarine purpose dialogue run inside this chapter?** (A) BOTH — Elarine early and short, Valeria at the end and deeper, giving the chapter a spine: the same question asked twice, and the second asking costs because by then he knows what he is missing. *Recommended.* (B) VALERIA ONLY — cleaner, but 04 calls hers "later and deeper", and running it alone makes any later Elarine version read as a lesser repeat.
- **DECISION 2 (owed): is this where "Val" is first used?** Proposal: once, at the very end, **and she does not respond to it at all, and he cannot tell whether that was permission** — it stays a beat and does not become sentiment. Alternative: hold it for the summit or the fire.

## SESSION 25 (2026-08-22) — CH. 21 "THE EDGE" + CH. 22 "THE NAMES"

**OPEN — cold-read audit, four findings.** (1) **The storm-weeks were already behind us**: 05 banked them as the timeskip's lead instrument, but Ch. 12 fixes them *at the heart of the cold term* and Ch. 20 lands *at the end of* it, so they ran off-page inside Ch. 20's own gap. Author ruling: dropped, the skip runs forward only. (2) **Phantom "within three exchanges"** — Elarine names no threshold on the page; `git log -S` puts the phrase's first appearance at `f0ce5c1`, the Ch. 20 commit itself, so the ledgers were written off the design and not off the page. It stood in two files and one was an instruction (`03/elarine` carried it as standing state). Struck; the ledger follows the page. (3) **04's locked-chest queued thread** still read *STILL UNPLANTED* three sessions after the chest was planted — closed, text rolled verbatim, byte-identity verified against HEAD. (4) **A pointer to a §DYNAMIC-BLOCK NOTE** that was never written anywhere in the repo — struck. Also ruled at the open: **beat 17's birth collides with the tournament.**

**DESIGN — the author replaced the proposed shape wholesale, and the replacement was better.** I had proposed rendering the timeskip as deltas on Kael's road. The author's shape: **re-run the routine** — Ch. 3 / Ch. 8's dawn-Vask / daylight-Temple / lamplight-Neris form, run again years later at the top of its development. Four months pass with no survey register anywhere, and the last ordinary day of his life is legible *as* an ordinary day.

**RULINGS IN SESSION (twelve).** The register rises — *use conductivity*, move off the child-words to the science of the Sanctum, **and Neris knows it** · the pregnancy foregrounded · Severin at the arena · tournament structure (Gold 16 at 6/5/5, Silver for the Temple's rest, **the summit is the SEMIFINAL**, the provision holding a houseless winner's city) · **liberal rules** — sharp blades, unlimited resonance, *no mortal intent* — **with the First Master as the only enforcement** · **the threading is MANY threads, not one** · **capacity is not the explanation for Vask's superiority** (a Sanctum elite out-controls a gifted child; the purse only means his errors would be free) · the Vel are a ladder, not a caste bar · the princess is 14, unmatched and radiant · other schools send fighters only · Kael brings the twin shorts and fights conventional.

**AUTHOR-CAUGHT IN THE PASS — four, and all four were mine.**
- *"just kind of theatrical dialogue here"* — the dagger argument proved everything on the body and then **said it all out loud anyway**: Vask announcing the shape of his own conversation, and lecturing about a school he had just demonstrated. Cut to silence, four words, and *Get loose*. **Same disease as the s22 prompt-line note, in the father's voice.**
- *"who is Ledron?"* — a name that existed only in the sentence announcing its own disappearance. Now one of Ch. 18's two senior-cohort names, with a referent the reader has actually seen.
- *"he does bring his daggers tho?"* — *his hand where the knife was not* was a Ch. 7 echo written on autopilot and **untrue since Ch. 19**. Inverted: the hand finds it, and nobody asked him for it at the gate.
- *"it does kinda say one thread a lot"* — my own new prose contradicting the threading ruling in four places, ten minutes after I wrote the ruling.

**SPLIT.** The draft reached 7,437 across five directed additions; the author called it two chapters. Split at its own seam — the routine ends on Vask in the doorway, the arena opens on *The island filled.* **21 "The Edge" 4,831w** (dialogue 25%, 21.2 w/para, the lightest in the book); **22 "The Names" 2,331w** (dialogue 3% — quiet on purpose: the chapter in which Kael is surrounded by more people than he has ever seen and speaks to nobody).

**TOOL HYGIENE.** Two of my own new protected entries — the herald's call and the closing line — were delisted to unquoted form after a parity run showed them chasing ordinary prose (*of the island*, *had begun*) through half the manuscript. Earlier in the session a spurious three-word fragment was caught inside a correction note: retirement text goes in *italics*, never in quotes, or the parser adopts it.
